We evaluate state-of-the-art systems and find a consistent gap: text-only language models reliably recover the intended stress from context, yet TTS systems frequently fail to realize it in speech. We release the benchmark, evaluation framework, construction pipeline and a synthetic corpus to support future work on context-aware speech synthesis.

</details>


### [40] [Bridging Linguistic Gaps: Cross-Lingual Mapping in Pre-Training and Dataset for Enhanced Multilingual LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10590)
*Weihua Zheng,Chang Liu,Zhengyuan Liu,Xin Huang,Kui Wu,Muhammad Huzaifah Md Shahrin,Aiti Aw,Roy Ka-Wei Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出在预训练阶段引入跨语言映射任务，通过双向映射语言嵌入空间来增强跨语言对齐，同时保持单语流畅性，显著提升了多语言大模型在机器翻译、跨语言自然语言理解和问答任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大语言模型在跨语言任务上表现不佳，主要原因是高资源语言和低资源语言之间的数据不平衡，以及预训练中的单语偏见。现有方法如双语微调和对比对齐需要大量平行数据或存在不稳定性问题。

Method: 在预训练阶段引入跨语言映射任务，在LLM嵌入空间中进行语言间的双向映射，增强跨语言对齐而不损害单语流畅性。同时提出语言对齐系数来量化跨语言一致性，即使在数据有限的情况下也能稳健评估。

Result: 在机器翻译任务上获得高达11.9 BLEU点的提升，在跨语言问答任务上BERTScore-Precision提升6.72点，在跨语言自然语言理解任务上准确率提升超过5%，均优于现有的强大多语言基线模型。

Conclusion: 将跨语言目标纳入预训练阶段能够有效提升多语言大语言模型的性能，特别是在跨语言对齐方面。该方法为解决数据不平衡和单语偏见问题提供了有效途径，具有重要的实践意义。

Abstract: Multilingual Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle with cross-lingual tasks due to data imbalances between high-resource and low-resource languages, as well as monolingual bias in pre-training. Existing methods, such as bilingual fine-tuning and contrastive alignment, can improve cross-lingual performance, but they often require extensive parallel data or suffer from instability. To address these challenges, we introduce a Cross-Lingual Mapping Task during the pre-training phase, which enhances cross-lingual alignment without compromising monolingual fluency. Our approach bi-directionally maps languages within the LLM embedding space, improving both language generation and comprehension. We further propose a Language Alignment Coefficient to robustly quantify cross-lingual consistency, even in limited-data scenarios. Experimental results on machine translation (MT), cross-lingual natural language understanding (CLNLU), and cross-lingual question answering (CLQA) show that our model achieves gains of up to 11.9 BLEU points in MT, 6.72 points in CLQA BERTScore-Precision, and more than 5% in CLNLU accuracy over strong multilingual baselines. These findings highlight the potential of incorporating cross-lingual objectives into pre-training to improve multilingual LLMs.

</details>


### [41] [Computational Lesions in Multilingual Language Models Separate Shared and Language-specific Brain Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10627)
*Yang Cui,Jingyuan Sun,Yizheng Sun,Yifan Wang,Yunhao Zhang,Jixing Li,Shaonan Wang,Hongpeng Zhou,John Hale,Chengqing Zong,Goran Nenadic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过在多语言大语言模型中创建"计算性损伤"，验证了大脑多语言处理存在共享核心与语言特异性嵌入的混合架构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大脑如何支持不同语言处理是神经科学的基本问题，也是多语言人工智能的重要测试。虽然神经影像学已识别出跨语言的语言响应脑区，但无法直接揭示底层处理是共享的还是语言特异的。

Method: 使用六个多语言大语言模型作为可控系统，通过归零对跨语言重要或对特定语言特别重要的参数集创建"计算性损伤"。比较完整模型和损伤模型在预测英语、汉语和法语母语者（112名参与者）自然故事聆听期间fMRI响应的能力。

Result: 损伤共享核心参数使全脑编码相关性降低60.32%；语言特异性损伤保留了嵌入空间中的跨语言分离，但选择性地削弱了匹配母语的脑预测能力。结果支持共享主干与嵌入特化的混合架构。

Conclusion: 研究为多语言大脑-模型对齐提供了因果框架，支持大脑语言处理采用共享核心与语言特异性嵌入相结合的架构，而非完全共享或完全独立的处理系统。

Abstract: How the brain supports language across different languages is a basic question in neuroscience and a useful test for multilingual artificial intelligence. Neuroimaging has identified language-responsive brain regions across languages, but it cannot by itself show whether the underlying processing is shared or language-specific. Here we use six multilingual large language models (LLMs) as controllable systems and create targeted ``computational lesions'' by zeroing small parameter sets that are important across languages or especially important for one language. We then compare intact and lesioned models in predicting functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) responses during 100 minutes of naturalistic story listening in native English, Chinese and French (112 participants). Lesioning a compact shared core reduces whole-brain encoding correlation by 60.32% relative to intact models, whereas language-specific lesions preserve cross-language separation in embedding space but selectively weaken brain predictivity for the matched native language. These results support a shared backbone with embedded specializations and provide a causal framework for studying multilingual brain-model alignment.

</details>


### [42] [ProUIE: A Macro-to-Micro Progressive Learning Method for LLM-based Universal Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10633)
*Wenda Liu,Zhigang Song,Shuai Nie,Guangyao Liu,Lisung Chen,Binyu Yang,Yaran Chen,Peng Zhou,Hongzhen Wang,Yuchen Liu,Wenyue Hu,Jiaming Xu,Runyu Shi,Ying Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProUIE：一种无需外部信息的宏-微渐进学习框架，通过三阶段训练提升通用信息抽取性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的通用信息抽取方法通常依赖额外信息，增加了训练复杂度但收益有限。需要一种不引入外部信息就能提升UIE性能的方法。

Method: 提出ProUIE三阶段渐进学习框架：1) 宏观完全建模：按NER、RE、EE难度顺序在完整数据上学习统一抽取基础；2) 中观精简对齐：在采样数据上简化目标格式，使结构化输出更简洁可控；3) 微观深度探索：应用GRPO和逐步细粒度奖励在结构单元上引导探索。

Result: 在36个公开数据集上的实验表明，ProUIE持续提升统一抽取性能，在NER和RE任务上平均优于强指令调优基线，且使用更小的骨干模型。在大规模生产导向的信息抽取中进一步显示出明显优势。

Conclusion: ProUIE通过宏-微渐进学习框架，在不引入外部信息的情况下有效提升了通用信息抽取性能，为UIE提供了一种更简洁高效的训练范式。

Abstract: LLM-based universal information extraction (UIE) methods often rely on additional information beyond the original training data, which increases training complexity yet often yields limited gains. To address this, we propose ProUIE, a Macro-to-Micro progressive learning approach that improves UIE without introducing any external information. ProUIE consists of three stages: (i) macro-level Complete Modeling (CM), which learns NER, RE, and EE along their intrinsic difficulty order on the full training data to build a unified extraction foundation, (ii) meso-level Streamlined Alignment (SA), which operates on sampled data with simplified target formats, streamlining and regularizing structured outputs to make them more concise and controllable, and (iii) micro-level Deep Exploration (DE), which applies GRPO with stepwise fine-grained rewards (SFR) over structural units to guide exploration and improve performance. Experiments on 36 public datasets show that ProUIE consistently improves unified extraction, outperforming strong instruction-tuned baselines on average for NER and RE while using a smaller backbone, and it further demonstrates clear gains in large-scale production-oriented information extraction.

</details>


### [43] [Efficient Process Reward Modeling via Contrastive Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10660)
*Nakyung Lee,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CPMI方法，利用模型内部概率自动标注推理步骤奖励，显著降低人工标注和计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过程奖励模型(PRM)需要人工标注推理步骤奖励，成本高耗时长；自动化方法如蒙特卡洛估计计算资源需求大

Method: 提出对比点互信息(CPMI)方法，通过量化推理步骤与正确答案相对于硬负例的互信息增量，自动生成步骤级监督信号

Result: CPMI标注相比蒙特卡洛估计减少84%数据集构建时间和98%token生成，在过程级评估和数学推理基准上获得更高准确率

Conclusion: CPMI是一种高效自动奖励标注方法，显著降低标注成本和计算负担，同时保持高质量的过程监督

Abstract: Recent research has devoted considerable effort to verifying the intermediate reasoning steps of chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories using process reward models (PRMs) and other verifier models. However, training a PRM typically requires human annotators to assign reward scores to each reasoning step, which is both costly and time-consuming. Existing automated approaches, such as Monte Carlo (MC) estimation, also demand substantial computational resources due to repeated LLM rollouts. To overcome these limitations, we propose contrastive pointwise mutual information (CPMI), a novel automatic reward labeling method that leverages the model's internal probability to infer step-level supervision while significantly reducing the computational burden of annotating dataset. CPMI quantifies how much a reasoning step increases the mutual information between the step and the correct target answer relative to hard-negative alternatives. This contrastive signal serves as a proxy for the step's contribution to the final solution and yields a reliable reward. The experimental results show that CPMI-based labeling reduces dataset construction time by 84% and token generation by 98% compared to MC estimation, while achieving higher accuracy on process-level evaluations and mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [44] [HeceTokenizer: A Syllable-Based Tokenization Approach for Turkish Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10665)
*Senol Gulgonul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HeceTokenizer是一种基于音节的土耳其语分词器，利用土耳其语的六模式音系结构构建约8000个独特音节类型的封闭词汇表，无OOV问题。配合BERT-tiny编码器和分块检索策略，在TQuAD检索基准上Recall@5达到50.3%，优于使用200倍大模型的形态学基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土耳其语具有确定性的六模式音系结构，这为构建基于音节的分词器提供了理论基础。传统基于形态学的分词方法需要大量参数，而利用音系规律性可以构建更轻量、高效的模型。

Method: 1. 基于土耳其语的六模式音系结构构建HeceTokenizer，创建约8000个独特音节类型的封闭词汇表，无OOV问题；2. 使用BERT-tiny编码器（150万参数）在土耳其语维基百科子集上从头训练掩码语言建模任务；3. 采用细粒度分块检索策略进行检索评估。

Result: 在TQuAD检索基准上，HeceTokenizer达到50.3%的Recall@5，超过了使用200倍大模型（约3亿参数）的形态学基线（46.92%）。这表明基于音节的轻量模型在检索任务上具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 土耳其语的音系规律性为检索任务提供了强大且资源友好的归纳偏置。基于音节的分词方法能够在极小的模型规模下实现优异的检索性能，为低资源语言处理提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: HeceTokenizer is a syllable-based tokenizer for Turkish that exploits the deterministic six-pattern phonological structure of the language to construct a closed, out-of-vocabulary (OOV)-free vocabulary of approximately 8,000 unique syllable types. A BERT-tiny encoder (1.5M parameters) is trained from scratch on a subset of Turkish Wikipedia using a masked language modeling objective and evaluated on the TQuAD retrieval benchmark using Recall@5. Combined with a fine-grained chunk-based retrieval strategy, HeceTokenizer achieves 50.3% Recall@5, surpassing the 46.92% reported by a morphology-driven baseline that uses a 200 times larger model. These results suggest that the phonological regularity of Turkish syllables provides a strong and resource-light inductive bias for retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [45] [Learning and Enforcing Context-Sensitive Control for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10667)
*Mohammad Albinhassan,Pranava Madhyastha,Mark Law,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出自动从LLM交互中学习上下文敏感约束的框架，消除手动规范需求，使小模型实现完美约束遵从


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文敏感约束能克服上下文无关文法在保证生成有效性方面的局限，但通常需要手动规范，这需要专业知识且构成显著障碍

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 句法探索阶段收集多样化输出用于约束学习；2) 约束利用阶段在生成时强制执行学习到的规则

Result: 实验表明该方法使小模型(1B参数)能学习并完美遵从约束生成，性能优于更大模型和最先进的推理模型

Conclusion: 首次将上下文敏感文法学习与LLM生成结合，消除手动规范同时保持生成有效性

Abstract: Controlling the output of Large Language Models (LLMs) through context-sensitive constraints has emerged as a promising approach to overcome the limitations of Context-Free Grammars (CFGs) in guaranteeing generation validity. However, such constraints typically require manual specification -- a significant barrier demanding specialized expertise. We introduce a framework that automatically learns context-sensitive constraints from LLM interactions through a two-phase process: syntactic exploration to gather diverse outputs for constraint learning, followed by constraint exploitation to enforce these learned rules during generation. Experiments demonstrate that our method enables even small LLMs (1B parameters) to learn and generate with perfect constraint adherence, outperforming larger counterparts and state-of-the-art reasoning models. This work represents the first integration of context-sensitive grammar learning with LLM generation, eliminating manual specification while maintaining generation validity.

</details>


### [46] [QFS-Composer: Query-focused summarization pipeline for less resourced languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10687)
*Vuk Đuranović,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出QFS-Composer框架，通过查询分解、问题生成、问答和摘要生成提升低资源语言查询聚焦摘要的事实对齐，在斯洛文尼亚语上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在文本摘要方面表现良好，但在训练资源受限的语言中效果显著下降。查询聚焦摘要(QFS)在低资源语言中面临标注数据集和评估工具有限的挑战。

Method: 提出QFS-Composer框架，整合查询分解、问题生成、问答和抽象摘要生成，以改善摘要与用户意图的事实对齐。基于斯洛文尼亚语LLM开发斯洛文尼亚语QA和QG模型，并适配无参考摘要评估方法。

Result: 实证评估表明，QA引导的摘要流水线相比基线LLM在一致性和相关性方面有所提升。该工作为推进低资源语言的QFS建立了可扩展的方法论。

Conclusion: QFS-Composer框架有效解决了低资源语言查询聚焦摘要的挑战，通过集成多个组件改善了摘要的事实对齐，为其他低资源语言提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong performance in text summarization, yet their effectiveness drops significantly across languages with restricted training resources. This work addresses the challenge of query-focused summarization (QFS) in less-resourced languages, where labeled datasets and evaluation tools are limited. We present a novel QFS framework, QFS-Composer, that integrates query decomposition, question generation (QG), question answering (QA), and abstractive summarization to improve the factual alignment of a summary with user intent. We test our approach on the Slovenian language. To enable high-quality supervision and evaluation, we develop the Slovenian QA and QG models based on a Slovene LLM and adapt evaluation approaches for reference-free summary evaluation. Empirical evaluation shows that the QA-guided summarization pipeline yields improved consistency and relevance over baseline LLMs. Our work establishes an extensible methodology for advancing QFS in less-resourced languages.

</details>


### [47] [Attention Sinks as Internal Signals for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10697)
*Jakub Binkowski,Kamil Adamczewski,Tomasz Kajdanowicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SinkProbe：基于注意力汇（attention sinks）的新型幻觉检测方法，通过分析注意力分布从输入驱动到先验主导的转变来识别幻觉


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常产生幻觉（事实错误或缺乏输入支持的输出），现有基于注意力图的检测方法机制不明确，需要理论支撑的检测方法

Method: 提出SinkProbe方法，基于观察：幻觉与注意力汇（积累过多注意力质量的标记）密切相关，表明注意力从分布式、输入驱动的计算转变为压缩的、先验主导的计算。方法不仅计算注意力汇分数，还发现分类器偏好与具有大范数值向量相关的汇

Result: SinkProbe在多个流行数据集和LLM上取得最先进的结果，建立了先前方法与注意力汇之间的数学关系，证明它们隐式依赖于注意力汇

Conclusion: SinkProbe提供了一种基于理论的新型幻觉检测方法，通过注意力汇机制解释幻觉产生过程，为理解LLM幻觉提供了理论框架

Abstract: Large language models frequently exhibit hallucinations: fluent and confident outputs that are factually incorrect or unsupported by the input context. While recent hallucination detection methods have explored various features derived from attention maps, the underlying mechanisms they exploit remain poorly understood. In this work, we propose SinkProbe, a hallucination detection method grounded in the observation that hallucinations are deeply entangled with attention sinks - tokens that accumulate disproportionate attention mass during generation - indicating a transition from distributed, input-grounded attention to compressed, prior-dominated computation. Importantly, although sink scores are computed solely from attention maps, we find that the classifier preferentially relies on sinks whose associated value vectors have large norms. Moreover, we show that previous methods implicitly depend on attention sinks by establishing their mathematical relationship to sink scores. Our findings yield a novel hallucination detection method grounded in theory that produces state-of-the-art results across popular datasets and LLMs.

</details>


### [48] [Expect the Unexpected? Testing the Surprisal of Salient Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10724)
*Jessica Lin,Amir Zeldes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现全局显著实体比非显著实体具有更高的惊奇值，且显著实体作为提示时会降低周围内容的惊奇值，增强文档可预测性，这一效应在不同文体中存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于均匀信息密度假设的研究表明，虽然整体上信息分布相对均匀，但局部差异可能源于句法和语篇结构约束。然而，现有研究大多忽视了语篇参与者的相对显著性。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究语篇中实体的整体显著性如何与惊奇值相关。

Method: 使用70K手动标注的提及（涵盖16种英语文体）和一种新颖的最小对提示方法，研究实体显著性与惊奇值的关系。控制位置、长度和嵌套等混淆因素，分析显著实体对周围内容惊奇值的影响。

Result: 全局显著实体比非显著实体具有显著更高的惊奇值。当显著实体作为提示时，会系统性地降低周围内容的惊奇值，增强文档层面的可预测性。这种效应在不同文体中存在差异：在主题连贯的文本中最强，在对话语境中最弱。

Conclusion: 研究结果通过识别全局实体显著性作为塑造语篇信息分布的机制，完善了均匀信息密度竞争压力框架。显著性不仅影响实体本身的惊奇值，还通过降低周围内容的惊奇值来增强文档可预测性。

Abstract: Previous work examining the Uniform Information Density (UID) hypothesis has shown that while information as measured by surprisal metrics is distributed more or less evenly across documents overall, local discrepancies can arise due to functional pressures corresponding to syntactic and discourse structural constraints. However, work thus far has largely disregarded the relative salience of discourse participants. We fill this gap by studying how overall salience of entities in discourse relates to surprisal using 70K manually annotated mentions across 16 genres of English and a novel minimal-pair prompting method. Our results show that globally salient entities exhibit significantly higher surprisal than non-salient ones, even controlling for position, length, and nesting confounds. Moreover, salient entities systematically reduce surprisal for surrounding content when used as prompts, enhancing document-level predictability. This effect varies by genre, appearing strongest in topic-coherent texts and weakest in conversational contexts. Our findings refine the UID competing pressures framework by identifying global entity salience as a mechanism shaping information distribution in discourse.

</details>


### [49] [Self-Correcting RAG: Enhancing Faithfulness via MMKP Context Selection and NLI-Guided MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10734)
*Shijia Xu,Zhou Wu,Xiaolong Jia,Yu Wang,Kai Liu,April Xiaowen Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Correcting RAG框架，通过约束优化和路径规划解决RAG在复杂推理任务中的上下文利用率低和幻觉问题，在输入侧将上下文选择形式化为MMKP问题，输出侧引入NLI引导的MCTS机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管检索增强生成(RAG)扩展了大语言模型的知识边界，但在处理复杂推理任务时仍面临两大挑战：上下文利用率低和频繁产生幻觉。需要一种系统性的方法来优化检索和生成过程。

Method: 提出Self-Correcting RAG统一框架：1) 输入侧将上下文选择形式化为多维多选择背包问题(MMKP)，在严格token预算下最大化信息密度并去除冗余；2) 输出侧引入自然语言推理(NLI)引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)机制，利用测试时计算动态探索推理轨迹并验证生成答案的忠实性。

Result: 在六个多跳问答和事实核查数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著提高了复杂查询的推理准确性，同时有效减少了幻觉，优于现有强基线方法。

Conclusion: Self-Correcting RAG通过将检索和生成重新形式化为约束优化和路径规划问题，有效解决了RAG在复杂推理任务中的核心挑战，为提升RAG系统的可靠性和准确性提供了系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) substantially extends the knowledge boundary of large language models. However, it still faces two major challenges when handling complex reasoning tasks: low context utilization and frequent hallucinations. To address these issues, we propose Self-Correcting RAG, a unified framework that reformulates retrieval and generation as constrained optimization and path planning. On the input side, we move beyond traditional greedy retrieval and, for the first time, formalize context selection as a multi-dimensional multiple-choice knapsack problem (MMKP), thereby maximizing information density and removing redundancy under a strict token budget. On the output side, we introduce a natural language inference (NLI)-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) mechanism, which leverages test-time compute to dynamically explore reasoning trajectories and validate the faithfulness of generated answers. Experiments on six multi-hop question answering and fact-checking datasets demonstrate that our method significantly improves reasoning accuracy on complex queries while effectively reducing hallucinations, outperforming strong existing baselines.Our code is available at https://github.com/xjiacs/Self-Correcting-RAG .

</details>


### [50] [BlasBench: An Open Benchmark for Irish Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10736)
*Jyoutir Raj,John Conway*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 发布BlasBench爱尔兰语ASR基准测试框架，评估12个系统在Common Voice和FLEURS数据集上的表现，发现Whisper变体WER超过100%，最佳开放模型omniASR LLM 7B在两个数据集上分别达到30.65%和39.09% WER，揭示基于单一数据集微调的模型存在泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏爱尔兰语特定的基准测试来在共享的爱尔兰语感知评估协议下比较端到端ASR系统，需要解决这一问题以促进爱尔兰语ASR研究。

Method: 发布BlasBench开放评估框架，包含爱尔兰语感知的文本规范化（保留重音符号、辅音弱化和元音同化），在Common Voice ga-IE和FLEURS ga-IE数据集上评估12个系统，涵盖四种架构家族。

Result: 所有Whisper变体WER超过100%；最佳开放模型omniASR LLM 7B在Common Voice上达到30.65% WER，在FLEURS上达到39.09% WER；在Common Voice上微调的模型在FLEURS上WER下降33-43个百分点，显示单一数据集评估无法揭示的泛化差距。

Conclusion: BlasBench填补了爱尔兰语ASR评估的空白，揭示了现有模型在爱尔兰语识别上的严重不足，特别是跨数据集泛化能力差的问题，强调了多数据集评估的重要性。

Abstract: No open Irish-specific benchmark compares end-user ASR systems under a shared Irish-aware evaluation protocol. To solve this, we release BlasBench, an open evaluation harness with Irish-aware text normalisation that preserves fadas, lenition, and eclipsis. We benchmark 12 systems across four architecture families on Common Voice ga-IE and FLEURS ga-IE. All Whisper variants exceed 100% WER. The best open model (omniASR LLM 7B) achieves 30.65% WER on Common Voice and 39.09% on FLEURS. We noticed models fine-tuned on Common Voice lose 33-43 WER points on FLEURS, revealing a generalisation gap that is invisible to single-dataset evaluation.

</details>

### [51] [ProxiCBO: A Provably Convergent Consensus-Based Method for Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09789)
*Haoyu Zhang,Yanting Ma,Ruangrawee Kitichotku,Joshua Rapp,Petros Boufounos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出ProxiCBO方法，将共识优化与近端梯度技术结合，用于解决信号处理中的非凸复合优化问题，在精度和粒子效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号处理中广泛存在可能非凸的复合优化问题，现有方法在处理复杂优化景观和利用目标函数复合结构方面存在局限，需要开发更有效的优化方法。

Method: 提出ProxiCBO方法，将共识优化(CBO)与近端梯度技术集成，处理具有挑战性的优化景观并利用目标函数的复合结构。建立了连续时间有限粒子动力学的全局收敛保证，并开发了交替更新方案用于高效实际实现。

Result: 在信号处理任务中的仿真结果表明，包括从一位量化测量中恢复信号和从单光子激光雷达数据中估计参数，ProxiCBO在精度和粒子效率方面均优于现有的近端梯度方法和CBO方法。

Conclusion: ProxiCBO方法成功解决了信号处理中的非凸复合优化问题，通过结合共识优化和近端梯度技术，提供了有效的优化框架，在多个实际应用中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: This paper introduces an interacting-particle optimization method tailored to possibly non-convex composite optimization problems, which arise widely in signal processing. The proposed method, \emph{ProxiCBO}, integrates consensus-based optimization (CBO) with proximal gradient techniques to handle challenging optimization landscapes and exploit the composite structure of the objective function. We establish global convergence guarantees for the continuous-time finite-particle dynamics and develop an alternating update scheme for efficient practical implementation. Simulation results for signal processing tasks, including signal recovery from one-bit quantized measurements and parameter estimation from single-photon lidar data, demonstrate that ProxiCBO outperforms existing proximal gradient methods and CBO methods in terms of both accuracy and particle-efficiency.

</details>


### [52] [Some properties of sets, functions, and multi-objective optimization problems using p-convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09882)
*Cristian Vera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了n维欧几里得空间中集合和函数的p-凸性概念，建立了该广义凸性框架下的代数与拓扑结果，并分析了多目标优化问题，重点研究了在分量函数p-凸性假设下的弱有效最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究p-凸性这一广义凸性概念，旨在扩展传统凸性理论，为多目标优化问题提供更广泛的分析框架，特别是在分量函数具有p-凸性而非传统凸性的情况下。

Method: 在n维欧几里得空间中定义集合和函数的p-凸性概念，建立相关的代数与拓扑性质理论，然后将该框架应用于多目标优化问题，重点分析弱有效最小值及其解集的特征。

Result: 建立了p-凸性框架下的新颖代数与拓扑结果，推导了在分量函数p-凸性假设下多目标优化问题弱有效最小值解集的若干特征和性质。

Conclusion: p-凸性为广义凸性分析提供了有效的理论框架，能够处理更广泛的多目标优化问题，特别是在分量函数仅满足p-凸性而非传统凸性的情况下，相关结果为这类问题的解集分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the concept of p-convexity for sets and functions in n-dimensional Euclidean space. We establish novel algebraic and topological results within this generalized convexity framework. Furthermore, we analyze multi-objective optimization problems, with a particular emphasis on weakly efficient minima, under the assumption of $p$-convexity of the component functions. Several characterizations and properties of the corresponding solution sets are derived.

</details>


### [53] [Convex Synthesis of First-Order Methods for Time-Varying Smooth Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09926)
*Bryan Van Scoy,Gianluca Bianchin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于鲁棒控制合成的框架，用于设计适用于时变光滑强凸问题的优化算法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态优化算法在动态环境中表现不佳，即使渐近也产生次优决策，需要专门针对时变问题的算法设计方法

Method: 结合静态设置中的凸鲁棒控制合成和内模原理，将底层时变性的模型直接嵌入到设计的算法中

Result: 开发了一个系统化的框架来设计适用于时变光滑强凸问题的一阶方法

Conclusion: 通过将鲁棒控制理论与内模原理结合，为时变优化问题提供了系统化的算法设计方法

Abstract: Time-varying optimization is fundamental to decision-making in dynamic environments, where objectives evolve over time due to exogenous signals or data streams. However, algorithms designed for static problems yield suboptimal decisions in dynamic scenarios, even asymptotically. In this paper, we develop a robust control synthesis framework to systematically design first-order methods for smooth strongly convex problems that vary in time. Our approach leverages both convex robust control synthesis in the static setting and the internal model principle by directly embedding a model of the underlying variability into the designed algorithm.

</details>


### [54] [A simulation-optimization approach for fractional, profitability-oriented inventory control under service-level type constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10012)
*Tianxiao Sun,Noah Schwarzkopf*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出数据驱动的库存决策框架，结合随机模拟与利润导向优化，在不确定性下提升库存管理效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物流中库存管理面临成本效率与可靠服务之间的平衡问题，传统模型常忽视库存投资与财务绩效的直接联系

Method: 结合随机模拟和利润导向优化：模拟阶段生成多场景性能估计，提供支出、收入和服务可靠性数据；分数优化过程寻找最高财务回报策略；算法通过模拟结果与优化反馈迭代优化参数

Result: 代表性业务场景的计算实验证实该方法能同时提升服务一致性和财务收益

Conclusion: 该框架为企业提供了实用的数据驱动路径，将运营响应能力与可持续盈利能力相结合

Abstract: Managing stock efficiently remains a core issue in modern logistics, where companies must reconcile cost efficiency with dependable service despite unpredictable market conditions. Conventional models often overlook the direct connection between investment in inventory and overall financial performance. This study introduces a data-driven decision framework that combines stochastic simulations with a profit-oriented optimization routine to enhance decision-making under uncertainty. The simulation stage generates performance estimates across multiple operating scenarios, providing realistic data on expenditures, revenues, and service reliability. These outcomes inform a fractional optimization process that searches for policies yielding the highest financial returns while maintaining required availability levels. The algorithm iteratively refines parameter values through feedback between simulated outcomes and optimization results, ensuring adaptability to dynamic enterprise systems. Computational experiments using representative business settings confirm that this approach improves both service consistency and financial yield. Overall, the framework demonstrates a practical, data-driven path for firms seeking to align operational responsiveness with sustainable profitability.

</details>


### [55] [A Bifidelity Proximal Quasi-Newton Method for Dense Rigid Body Suspension Collision Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10089)
*Nicholas Rummel,Tyler Jensen,Stephen Becker,Eduardo Corona*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Mono-PQN和Bi-PQN方法，高效求解稠密刚性体悬浮液模拟中的线性互补问题，显著加速碰撞分辨率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密刚性体悬浮液的直接数值模拟面临巨大计算挑战，特别是每个时间步需要求解线性互补问题(LCP)，而LCP中的每个矩阵向量乘积都需要求解昂贵的偏微分方程

Method: 开发了定制化的单保真度近端拟牛顿法(Mono-PQN)和双保真度变体(Bi-PQN)，通过优化LCP求解过程，通常仅需3-4次矩阵向量乘积

Conclusion: 提出的Mono-PQN和Bi-PQN方法能高效求解稠密悬浮液模拟中的LCP问题，Bi-PQN尤其表现出鲁棒且与问题规模无关的收敛特性，显著提升计算效率

Abstract: Direct numerical simulation of dense rigid body suspensions poses significant computational challenges. A popular approach to resolve collisions necessitates solving a linear complementary problem (LCP) per time step. Each matrix vector product (MVP) inside the LCP requires solving an expensive partial differential equation. In this work, we show the LCP can be solved efficiently, often in only three to four MVPs. Specifically, we develop a custom monofidelity proximal quasi-Newton (Mono-PQN) method and a bi-fidelity variant (Bi-PQN). Our approach is validated through an application to representative systems of dense Stokesian Janus particles. Notably, in contact resolution our Mono-PQN and Bi-PQN achieve $\approx 1.5 \times$ and $> 2 \times$ speed up respectively against a competitive baseline, with the latter method displaying robust, problem-size-independent convergence. For our largest simulation involving $216$ particles, our Bi-PQN cut total simulation runtime to five days, as compared to the eight days required by the prior state-of-the-art method.

Abstract: Byzantine-robust distributed optimization relies on robust aggregation rules to mitigate the influence of malicious Byzantine workers. Despite the proliferation of such rules, a unified convergence analysis framework that accommodates general data heterogeneity is lacking. In this work, we provide a thorough convergence theory of Byzantine-robust distributed stochastic gradient descent (SGD), analyzing variants both with and without local momentum. We establish the convergence rates for nonconvex smooth objectives and those satisfying the Polyak-Lojasiewicz condition under a general data heterogeneity assumption. Our analysis reveals that while stochasticity and data heterogeneity introduce unavoidable error floors, local momentum provably reduces the error component induced by stochasticity. Furthermore, we derive matching lower bounds to demonstrate that the upper bounds obtained in our analysis are tight and characterize the fundamental limits of Byzantine resilience under stochasticity and data heterogeneity. Empirical results support our theoretical findings.

</details>

Conclusion: 通过引入人工粘性正则化，成功解决了无限时域折扣控制问题中策略迭代的不适定性问题。所提出的半离散方法不仅恢复了比较原理和单调性，还建立了精确的收敛性分析和误差估计，为实际计算提供了理论保证。该方法揭示了与有限时域情况根本不同的收敛机制。

Abstract: We study policy iteration (PI) for deterministic infinite-horizon discounted optimal control problems, whose value function is characterized by a stationary Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) equation. At the PDE level, PI is fundamentally ill-posed: the improvement step requires pointwise evaluation of $\nabla V$, which is not well defined for viscosity solutions, and thus the associated nonlinear operator cannot be interpreted in a stable functional sense. We develop a monotone semi-discrete formulation for the stationary discounted setting by introducing a space-discrete scheme with artificial viscosity of order $O(h)$. This regularization restores comparison, ensures monotonicity of the discrete operator, and yields a well-defined pointwise policy improvement via discrete gradients. Our analysis reveals a convergence mechanism fundamentally different from the finite-horizon case. For each fixed mesh size $h>0$, we prove that the semi-discrete PI sequence converges monotonically and geometrically to the unique discrete solution, where the contraction is induced by the resolvent structure of the discounted operator. We further establish the sharp vanishing-viscosity estimate $\|V^h - V\|_{L^\infty} \leq C\sqrt{h}$, and derive a quantitative error decomposition that separates policy iteration error from discretization error, exhibiting a nontrivial coupling between iteration count and mesh size. Numerical experiments in nonlinear one and two-dimensional control problems confirm the theoretical predictions, including geometric convergence and the characteristic decay-then-plateau behavior of the total error.

</details>

Method: 提出两个主要思想：1）统一的主控化框架用于推导和分析松弛；2）新颖的基于缩放技术的界增强方法（双缩放）。利用主控化对称化谱函数并得到其凸包络，在log-determinant设置下建立双缩放理论性质。

Result: 理论证明双缩放严格优于已知缩放界；首次推导出linx和Γ分解松弛之间的形式支配关系及其缩放增强变体之间的关系；数值结果显示双缩放linx松弛在界质量和计算效率上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的统一主控化框架和双缩放技术为MESP提供了系统化的松弛改进方法，双缩放linx松弛在理论和数值上都表现出优越性能，为最优实验设计和信息获取提供了更有效的近似解。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the maximum entropy sampling problem (MESP) and its variants. MESP seeks to identify a small subset of variables that maximizes the determinant of a covariance submatrix, and is a fundamental model in optimal experimental design and information acquisition. Although MESP is combinatorial and NP-hard, continuous relaxations, most notably linx and $Γ$ factorization, provide tractable approximations, yet their derivation, relative strength, and potential for systematic improvement remain poorly understood. We address this gap by introducing two main ideas: a unified majorization-based framework for deriving and analyzing relaxations, and a novel scaling-based bound-enhancement technique, which we call double-scaling. Our approach is motivated by the observation that the difficulty of MESP arises from two distinct sources: the combinatorial selection structure and the lack of permutation symmetry in the spectral objective. Majorization naturally resolves the latter by symmetrizing the spectral function and yielding its convex envelope. In the log-determinant setting, we establish the main theoretical properties of double-scaling and prove that it strictly dominates previously known scaling bounds. Using our majorization-based alternative characterization of $Γ$ factorization relaxation, we also derive, for the first time, formal dominance relations between linx- and $Γ$ factorization-bounds, as well as between their scaling-strengthened variants. Our numerical results show that our double-scaled linx relaxation consistently and substantially outperforms existing scaling methods and compares quite favorably with other state-of-the-art relaxations in terms of both bound quality and computational efficiency.

</details>

Method: 1) 平滑随机重排引入的离散噪声，利用连续状态马尔可夫链理论建立新颖的大数定律和中心极限定理；2) 使用谱张量技术证明即使在使用重排引入的有偏梯度估计器时，外推法也能消除偏差并锐化渐近行为。

Result: 组合方法不仅保持增强的均方误差保证，还产生更大的偏差三次细化。实验验证了理论，在实践中持续显示出显著的加速效果。

Conclusion: 随机重排与Richardson-Romberg外推法的组合在结构化非单调变分不等式问题中产生严格优于单独使用任一方法的协同效应，为这种组合提供了首个理论保证。

Abstract: From adversarial robustness to multi-agent learning, many machine learning tasks can be cast as finite-sum min-max optimization or, more generally, as variational inequality problems (VIPs). Owing to their simplicity and scalability, stochastic gradient methods with constant step size are widely used, despite the fact that they converge only up to a constant term. Among the many heuristics adopted in practice, two classical techniques have recently attracted attention to mitigate this issue: \emph{Random Reshuffling} of data and \emph{Richardson--Romberg extrapolation} across iterates. Random Reshuffling sharpens the mean-squared error (MSE) of the estimated solution, while Richardson-Romberg extrapolation acts orthogonally, providing a second-order reduction in its bias. In this work, we show that their composition is strictly better than both, not only maintaining the enhanced MSE guarantees but also yielding an even greater cubic refinement in the bias. To the best of our knowledge, our work provides the first theoretical guarantees for such a synergy in structured non-monotone VIPs. Our analysis proceeds in two steps: (i) we smooth the discrete noise induced by reshuffling and leverage tools from continuous-state Markov chain theory to establish a novel law of large numbers and a central limit theorem for its iterates; and (ii) we employ spectral tensor techniques to prove that extrapolation debiases and sharpens the asymptotic behavior even under the biased gradient oracle induced by reshuffling. Finally, extensive experiments validate our theory, consistently demonstrating substantial speedups in practice.

</details>

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了后向随机线性二次控制模型的普适性，为金融投资组合优化和风险管理等动态优化问题提供了新的分析框架。创新点在于解决了交叉项成本函数带来的结构不对称问题，建立了完整的理论体系。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the discrete-time backward stochastic linear quadratic (BSLQ) optimal control problem with nonhomogeneous system terms and cost function cross terms. The terminal constraint of such systems distinguishes it from forward stochastic systems, posing unique challenges for analysis and solution. Within the Hilbert space framework, we first clarify the necessary and sufficient conditions for problem solvability, then introduce the backward stochastic system maximum principle to derive the Hamiltonian system characterizing the optimal control. After equivalent transformation of the original problem, we use the decoupling method to obtain the corresponding Riccati equation, and present the explicit state feedback expression of the optimal control and the analytical form of the value function. Finally, numerical examples verify the effectiveness and feasibility of the proposed method. The innovation lies in expanding the model generality: addressing the structural asymmetry issue in the Riccati equation with cross-term cost functions, we propose system equivalent transformation and decoupling techniques. Our theoretical results provide a new analytical framework for dynamic optimization problems such as financial portfolio optimization and risk management.

</details>

Conclusion: 该统一压缩算法框架为分布式非凸优化提供了通用的压缩解决方案，支持多种压缩器类型，在保持通信效率的同时保证了收敛性能，特别在局部有界压缩器类中取得了与集中式算法相当的收敛率。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a unified compression algorithm for distributed nonconvex opitmization with both the locally- and globally-bounded communication compressors, including 1-bit compressors, saturating quantizers, and the globally-bounded compressors with both relative and absolute compression errors, as well as additional arbitrary bounded noise. We provide a rigorous convergence analysis in nonconvex settings and establish linear convergence under the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (P-L) condition. Notably, we establish an $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence rate for the locally-bounded class in the distributed nonconvex setting, matching that achieved by the centralized algorithms with 1-bit compressors, where $T$ denotes the total number of iterations. Moreover, one initial uncompressed communication round further yields an order-wise improvement to $\mathcal{O}(1/T^{2/3})$. For the P-L setting and the globally-bounded class, we recover state-of-the-art convergence rates.

Abstract: We investigate the robust Stackelberg null controllability of a one-dimensional forward linear stochastic Kuramoto--Sivashinsky--Korteweg--de Vries (KS--KdV) equation. The control framework is formulated as a hierarchical Stackelberg game involving two leaders, one follower, and worst-case disturbances acting in both the drift and diffusion terms. The first leader acts to drive the system to rest, while the second leader is introduced to overcome analytical difficulties arising from the stochastic setting. The follower, by reducing the effect of the disturbances, addresses a tracking-type control problem aimed at keeping the system state and its first and second spatial derivatives close to prescribed target trajectories. First, the robust control problem is characterized by the existence of a saddle point. Then, the analysis is reduced to the null controllability of a strongly coupled forward--backward stochastic KS--KdV system. The problem is addressed by combining a duality technique with new Carleman estimates for forward and backward stochastic fourth-order parabolic equations.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study the convergence of the last iterate (i.e., the $(N+1)$-th iterate) of the AdaGrad method. Although AdaGrad -- an adaptive subgradient method -- underpins a wide class of algorithms, most existing convergence analyses focus on averaged (or best) iterates. We derive worst-case upper bounds on the suboptimality of the final point and show that, with an optimally tuned stepsize parameter, the last iterate converges at the rate $O(1/N^{1/4})$. We complement this guarantee with matching lower-bound constructions, proving that this rate is tight and that AdaGrad's last-iterate rate is strictly worse than the classical $O(1/N^{1/2})$ rate for its averaged iterate. Technically, our analysis introduces an exponent parameter that captures the growth of the cumulative squared subgradients; combined with the last-iterate inequality of Zamani and Glineur (2025), this reduces the problem to bounding a particular series.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二阶控制系统能否通过反馈控制来模拟规定的一阶动力系统的行为，这在机器人学、力学系统控制等领域有重要应用价值，旨在理解高阶系统能否有效模拟低阶动力学。

Method: 采用几何控制理论方法，将向量场视为切丛的截面，研究二阶系统（包括大多数拉格朗日系统）能否通过反馈控制使规定的一阶向量场渐近稳定。分析完全驱动和欠驱动两种情况的差异。

Result: 完全驱动系统可实现全局指数稳定：任何光滑向量场对应的截面都能被全局指数稳定，且具有法向双曲性，闭环解不仅渐近具有规定速度，还以指数速率收敛到规定向量场生成的一阶动力学轨迹。欠驱动系统在欧拉特征非零流形上，几乎所有光滑向量场（特别是只有孤立零点的向量场）无法局部渐近稳定。

Conclusion: 二阶系统能否模拟规定一阶动力学取决于驱动程度和流形拓扑：完全驱动系统可实现全局指数稳定模拟，而欠驱动系统在欧拉特征非零流形上存在根本性限制，欧拉特征假设对负面结果是必要的。

Abstract: We study whether second-order systems can be made to behave like prescribed first-order dynamical systems through feedback control. More precisely, we study whether prescribed vector fields on compact smooth manifolds, viewed geometrically as sections of the tangent bundle, can be asymptotically stabilized in a strong sense by second-order control systems on the base manifold. Our class of second-order systems includes most Lagrangian systems, and we obtain both positive and negative results. The positive result asserts that, for fully actuated systems, the section corresponding to any smooth vector field can be made globally exponentially stable, normally hyperbolic, and more. In particular, not only does each closed-loop solution asymptotically have the prescribed velocities, but it also converges to a trajectory of the first-order dynamics generated by the prescribed vector field at an exponential rate. Thus, the closed-loop second-order system asymptotically reproduces the prescribed first-order dynamics. In contrast, the negative result asserts that, for underactuated systems on manifolds with nonzero Euler characteristic, sections corresponding to "almost all" smooth vector fields cannot even be locally asymptotically stabilized. This includes, in particular, all vector fields with only isolated zeros. An example shows that the Euler characteristic assumption is necessary for the negative result.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有连续扰动的极大单调算子驱动的微分包含问题的数值解法，这类问题在摩擦系统等力学应用中常见，需要发展能处理约束和单调性的稳健数值方法。

Method: 将单调算子分解为其单值部分的闭凸包和闭凸集法锥之和，基于此建立解的存在性；提出变步长时间离散化追赶算法，使用近似投影，通过离散速度分解和离散能量不等式分析收敛性。

Result: 在温和的切向耗散假设下获得全局能量界；在局部Lipschitz扰动下得到唯一性和初值稳定性；证明离散轨迹在有限时域上收敛于连续解；获得迭代的一致有界性、定量稳定性估计和显式误差界；建立预测步的L²渐近可行性和Cesàro型平均可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的追赶算法能有效求解具有连续扰动的单调算子微分包含问题，理论分析为数值实现提供了坚实基础，并在一维和多维约束干摩擦系统中得到验证。

Abstract: We study a catching-up algorithm for a class of differential inclusions driven by maximal monotone operators with continuous perturbations. Using a decomposition of the monotone operator into the closed convex hull of its single-valued part and the normal cone to a closed convex set, we establish existence of solutions and derive global energy bounds under a mild tangent dissipativity assumption. Under an additional local Lipschitz assumption on the perturbation, we also obtain uniqueness and stability with respect to the initial data. We then analyze a time-discretized catching-up scheme with variable step sizes and approximate projections. On every finite horizon, we prove convergence of the discrete trajectories to solutions of the continuous problem. A discrete velocity decomposition together with a discrete energy inequality yields uniform boundedness of the iterates, quantitative stability estimates, and explicit error bounds. We also establish asymptotic feasibility of the predictor step in an $L^2$ sense, as well as a Cesàro-type averaged feasibility property, showing that the constraint violations generated by the free step vanish as the discretization is refined. Finally, we illustrate the theory on explicit examples, including a fully explicit one--dimensional test case and a multidimensional constrained dry-friction system.

</details>


### [66] [Pseudoconvex Problems in Operational Decision Systems: Algorithms for Joint Learning and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10778)
*Zijun Li,Aswin Kannan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个同时学习与优化的框架，用于解决具有伪凸结构的多目标实时决策问题，通过迭代更新内外层变量实现收敛算法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注凸的、基于收益的目标函数，但在实际决策系统中需要扩展到多目标优化，特别是在能源系统中需要考虑可再生能源渗透率和发电成本等指标。同时，许多实际问题具有伪凸结构，如能源管理中的分数目标和零售中的logit收益模型

Method: 提出一个同时学习与优化的框架，外层优化伪凸目标函数，内层使用历史数据训练机器学习模型。开发了迭代更新内外层变量的收敛算法，在现实的数学假设下保证算法收敛性

Result: 使用真实世界数据集评估计算性能，发现了一个重要观察：在评估最终解质量时，不精确学习与计算时间之间存在明显的权衡关系

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了实时决策系统的优化和学习问题到多目标和伪凸结构，提出的框架和算法在实际应用中具有良好性能，揭示了学习精度与计算效率之间的权衡关系

Abstract: We consider joint optimization and learning problems arising in real-time decision systems. While most existing work focuses primarily on convex, revenue-based objectives, we extend this line of research to multi-objective formulations. In energy systems, for instance, we incorporate metrics such as renewable penetration and generation costs. Our key focus, however, is on a class of problems with a pseudoconvex structure - a natural relaxation of convexity. Representative examples include fractional objectives in energy management and logit-based revenue models in retail. The outer-level problem optimizes these pseudoconvex objectives, while the inner-level problem involves training a machine learning model using historical data. Our contributions are twofold. First, we propose a simultaneous learning-and-optimization framework that iteratively updates both inner- and outer-level variables. Second, we develop convergent algorithms for these problem classes under realistic mathematical assumptions. Using real-world datasets, we evaluate the computational performance of our methods and highlight an important observation: there exist clear trade-offs between inexact learning and computational time when assessing final solution quality.

</details>


### [67] [A study of column generation embedded in scalarization methods for the bi-objective cutting stock problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10850)

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究多目标切割问题，提出基于动态列生成的标量化方法，比较三种方法在帕累托前沿近似上的表现，为工业应用提供计算工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标组合优化和切割问题已有广泛研究，但多目标切割问题关注有限。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究一维和二维切割问题的双目标优化，为工业应用提供实用解决方案。

Method: 采用基于动态列生成的标量化方法，嵌入到三种从文献中改编的方法中，分析总物体数量最小化和总锯切周期最小化之间的权衡关系。

Result: 计算结果表明，动态列生成能提供更好的帕累托前沿近似；三种标量化方法没有明显优劣，可视为互补；通过方法联合生成的帕累托前沿近似在基数和超体积上均优于单个方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为处理多目标切割问题提供了见解和计算工具，有助于工业界获得实用解决方案，动态列生成方法在多目标优化中表现出色，多种方法的联合使用能获得更全面的帕累托前沿。

Abstract: Research on multi-objective combinatorial optimization and on the Cutting Stock Problem (CSP) has been widely developed over the years. In contrast, the multi-objective Cutting Stock Problem has received limited attention and has been explored in only a small number of studies. In this paper a bi-objective study of the one-dimensional and the two-dimensional CSP is presented. It distinguishes itself from other research in the literature in two key aspects, among others. The first regards the model used to represent the problem and the second is the solution strategy based on dynamic column generation embedded into scalarization methods. Three methods adapted from the literature to analyse the trade-off between the minimization of the total number of objects and the total number of saw cycles are implemented. The computational results show that the use of dynamic column generation provides a better approximation of the Pareto front. As for the scalarization methods, none stood out. In fact, they can be viewed as complementary. The cardinality and hypervolume of an approximation of the Pareto front built from the union of the points generated by the three methods is always greater than the ones generated by each one. Dealing with the multi-objective nature of CSP, this study provides insights and computational tools to help industry obtain pragmatic solutions.

</details>


### [68] [Sample Average Approximation for Distributionally Robust Optimization with $φ$-divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10855)
*Yan Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了在φ-散度球内评估随机变量最坏情况期望的样本复杂度，发现其是否依赖于名义分布P取决于散度函数φ的增长特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的有界随机变量期望估计具有独立于概率分布的样本复杂度O(B²/ε²)，但作者想知道在考虑最坏情况期望（在φ-散度球内）时，这种独立性是否仍然成立。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究了当概率测度属于以名义测度P为中心的φ-散度球时，评估随机变量最坏情况期望的样本复杂度。分析了散度函数φ的增长特性对样本复杂度的影响。

Result: 当φ-散度函数呈现超线性增长时，可以通过样本平均近似获得独立于P的样本复杂度O(M_{φ,τ}(ε)/ε²)；当φ不具有超线性增长时，任何估计方法的最坏情况期望评估都具有依赖于P的样本复杂度下界，且可以通过改变P使其任意大。

Conclusion: 最坏情况期望估计的样本复杂度是否独立于名义分布P完全取决于φ-散度函数的增长特性：超线性增长保证独立性，否则必然存在对P的依赖。

Abstract: It is well known that estimating the expectation of any given bounded random variable with values in $[-B, B]$ has a sample complexity of $\mathcal{O}(B^2/ε^2)$ that is independent of the underlying probability measure. We show that this property can no longer hold when evaluating the worst-case expectation of the random variable, when the probability measures defining the expectation belong to the $φ$-divergence ball centered at some nominal measure $P$. Specifically, the sample complexity and its dependence on the nominal measure can be completely characterized by the growth of the divergence function. When the divergence function $φ$ exhibits superlinear growth, then a $P$-independent sample complexity of $\mathcal{O}(M_{φ, τ}(ε) / ε^2)$ can be obtained by sample average approximation. Here $M_{φ, τ}(\cdot) $ is a function that only depends on the growth of $φ$ and the radius $τ$ of the divergence ball. On the other hand, when superlinear growth does not hold for $φ$, we show that for any estimation method, evaluating the worst-case expectation has a $P$-dependent sample complexity lower bound that can be made arbitrarily large by changing $P$.

</details>


### [69] [Stochastic Modified Equations for Stochastic Gradient Descent in Infinite-Dimensional Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10860)

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无限维希尔伯特空间中随机梯度下降(SGD)的连续时间极限，证明了离散动力学可由圆柱布朗运动驱动的随机微分方程(SDE)近似，并将扩散近似结果从欧几里得空间推广到无限维设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算中的逆问题通常需要在无限维希尔伯特空间中进行优化，SGD是此类设置中常用的求解器。然而，现有扩散近似结果主要局限于欧几里得空间，需要将其扩展到无限维设置以更好地理解SGD在函数空间中的行为。

Method: 研究小步长机制下SGD的连续时间极限，证明离散动力学可由圆柱布朗运动驱动的SDE近似。通过建立协方差算子的适当结构条件来处理圆柱噪声的适定性问题，并引入希尔伯特空间上的光滑泛函类，在弱意义下比较SGD与极限SDE的差异。

Result: 证明了SGD与极限SDE之间的差异（通过光滑泛函评估）是步长的二阶量。数值实验证实了预测的收敛行为，成功将扩散近似结果从欧几里得空间扩展到无限维希尔伯特空间。

Conclusion: 该工作成功建立了无限维希尔伯特空间中SGD的连续时间极限理论，解决了圆柱噪声适定性和弱收敛比较两个关键分析难点，为理解函数空间中的随机优化算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Inverse problems in scientific computing often require optimization over infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces. A commonly used solver in such settings is stochastic gradient descent (SGD), where gradients are approximated using randomly sampled sub-objective functions. In this work we study the continuous-time limit of SGD in the small step-size regime. We show that the discrete dynamics can be approximated by a stochastic differential equation (SDE) driven by cylindrical Brownian motion. The analysis extends diffusion-approximation results previously established in Euclidean spaces to the infinite-dimensional setting.
  Two analytical difficulties arise in this extension. First, the cylindrical nature of the noise requires establishing well-posedness of the resulting stochastic evolution equation through appropriate structural conditions on the covariance operator. Second, since the randomness in SGD originates from discrete sampling while the limiting equation is driven by Gaussian noise, the comparison between the two dynamics must be carried out in a weak sense. We therefore introduce a suitable class of smooth functionals on the Hilbert space and prove that the discrepancy between SGD and the limiting SDE, when evaluated through these functionals, is of second order in the step size. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence behavior.

</details>


### [70] [Nesterov Acceleration with Operator Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11105)
*Jaewook Lee,Ernest K. Ryu,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: NOD将Nesterov加速梯度下降扩展到强单调Lipschitz算子优化，通过算子分解和分解后oracle实现加速收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Nesterov加速梯度下降（NAG）从光滑强凸优化扩展到更广泛的强单调Lipschitz算子优化问题，解决传统方法无法直接应用的问题

Method: 提出Nesterov加速算子分解（NOD）方法：1）将算子分解为循环单调和单调分量（使用Asplund分解获得最紧表示）；2）算法利用分解后的oracle进行计算；3）扩展经典Nesterov加速理论

Result: 获得ε-精确解的迭代复杂度为Θ(√(L_φ/μ + L_𝕊²/μ²) log(1/ε))，其中μ是强单调参数，L_φ是循环单调分量的Lipschitz常数，L_𝕊是单调余项的Lipschitz常数

Conclusion: NOD成功将Nesterov加速扩展到强单调Lipschitz算子优化，通过算子分解实现加速收敛，统一并扩展了经典Nesterov加速理论

Abstract: We propose Nesterov acceleration with Operator Decomposition (NOD), which extends Nesterov's accelerated gradient descent (NAG) from smooth strongly convex optimization to the broader setting of strongly monotone, Lipschitz operators. The key insight is to decompose the operator into cyclically monotone and monotone components, with the Asplund decomposition providing the tightest such representation, and to have the algorithm utilize the decomposed oracles. NOD and its analysis subsume the classical theory of Nesterov acceleration and yield an iteration complexity for finding an $ε$-accurate solution of \[ Θ\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_φ}μ + \frac{L_{\mathbb{S}}^2}{μ^2}} \,\log \frac{1}ε\right), \] where $μ$ is the strong monotonicity parameter, $L_φ$ is the Lipschitz constant of the cyclically monotone component, and $L_{\mathbb{S}}$ is the Lipschitz constant of the (possibly acyclic) monotone remainder.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出基于凸优化和平方和技术的多项式矩阵函数多凸性验证方法，以及基于矩-SOS层次结构的多凸包络数值计算方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多凸性是确保非线性弹性和变分问题中极小值存在性的重要正则性条件，但判断给定能量是否多凸或计算多凸包络通常是困难问题

Method: 使用平方和(SOS)技术提供基于凸优化的充分条件来验证多凸性；基于多项式优化的矩-SOS层次结构开发点态计算多凸包络的数值方法

Result: 提出了多项式矩阵函数多凸性验证和包络计算的可处理数值框架，将困难的理论问题转化为可计算的凸优化问题

Conclusion: 该工作为多项式矩阵函数的多凸性分析和包络计算提供了实用的数值工具，有助于非线性弹性和变分问题的理论研究和实际应用

Abstract: A function of a matrix is polyconvex when it can be expressed as a convex function of the matrix minors. Polyconvexity is a regularity condition ensuring existence of minimizers in nonlinear elasticity and, more broadly, in vectorial problems of the calculus of variations, when minimizing integral gradient functionals. The polyconvex envelope of a function is the largest polyconvex lower bound. Yet deciding whether a given energy is polyconvex, or computing the polyconvex envelope, are generally difficult problems. This paper focuses on polynomial matrix functions. We propose (i) tractable convex-optimization based sufficient conditions to certify polyconvexity via sum-of-squares (SOS) technology, and (ii) a principled numerical method to compute the polyconvex envelope pointwise, based on the moment-SOS hierarchy from polynomial optimization.

</details>

Method: 考虑在每坐标上具有双井形状的支付函数，其中确定性动力学存在极限环。当两个坐标之间的耦合足够强且熵正则化足够小时，分析平均场动力学行为。

Result: 证明在强耦合和小熵正则化条件下，平均场动力学保持接近极限环的循环行为，因此不收敛。

Conclusion: 平均场Langevin下降-上升流在$\mathbb{R}^2$上的收敛性陈述存在反例，特定条件下动力学可能不收敛而呈现循环行为。

Abstract: We present a counterexample to the statement of convergence of the mean-field Langevin descent-ascent flow on $\mathbb{R}^2$. We consider payoff functions that are shaped as a double well in each coordinate, and for which the deterministic dynamics admits a limit cycle. When the coupling between the two coordinates is sufficiently strong and the entropic regularization sufficiently small, we show that the mean-field dynamics remains close to this cyclic behavior, and in particular, does not converge.

</details>


### [73] [Proximal Nonlinear Conjugate Gradient Methods for Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11150)
*Shodai Hamana,Yasushi Narushima*

TL;DR: 提出了一种近端非线性共轭梯度法，将非线性共轭梯度法扩展到复合目标函数（光滑非凸函数与非光滑凸函数之和），并进一步扩展到非光滑函数为弱凸的情况。

Motivation: 非线性共轭梯度法在标准无约束优化问题中非常有效，尤其适用于大规模问题。然而，传统方法无法直接处理复合目标函数（光滑项+非光滑项）的优化问题，需要扩展该方法以处理更广泛的优化问题类型。

Method: 使用前向-后向残差（基于近端映射而非梯度）定义搜索方向，采用三项Hestenes-Stiefel公式确定搜索方向。该方法可处理光滑非凸函数与非光滑凸函数之和的复合目标函数，并扩展到非光滑函数为弱凸的情况。

Result: 在标准假设下建立了全局收敛性（包括凸和弱凸非光滑函数）。当光滑项强凸时，给出了收敛速率分析。数值实验表明该方法稳定，在凸和非凸设置下均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的近端非线性共轭梯度法成功扩展了传统非线性共轭梯度法，能够有效处理复合目标函数优化问题，具有良好的理论保证和实际性能。

Abstract: The nonlinear conjugate gradient methods are known to be an effective approach for standard unconstrained optimization problems especially for large-scale problems. This paper proposes a proximal nonlinear conjugate gradient method, which extends the nonlinear conjugate gradient methods to composite objective functions, namely, the sum of a smooth nonconvex function and a nonsmooth convex function, and its extension to the case where the nonsmooth function is weakly convex. The proposed method uses the forward-backward residual which is defined by using the proximal mapping instead of the gradient and determines the search direction based on the three-term Hestenes-Stiefel (HS) formula. We establish global convergence under standard assumptions, both convex and weakly convex nonsmooth fuctions. In addition, we characterize the convergence rate when the smooth term is strongly convex. Finally, numerical experiments show that the proposed method is stable and achieves better performance than existing methods in both convex and nonconvex settings.

</details>

Method: 将间接反馈方法从线性随机MPC的机会约束扩展到条件风险价值等风险厌恶约束，建立递归可行性证明框架，基于随机耗散性概念和终端条件确保性能

Result: 建立了风险厌恶MPC方案的递归可行性和闭环约束满足性，基于随机耗散性和合适的终端条件确保了闭环平均性能的（近）最优性

Conclusion: 成功将随机MPC从机会约束扩展到风险厌恶约束，提供了理论保证框架，能更好控制尾部风险同时保持性能最优性

Abstract: Chance constraints are widely used in stochastic model predictive control (MPC) to enforce probabilistic state and input constraints in the presence of unbounded disturbances. However, they only restrict violation probabilities and do not account for the magnitude of rare but severe constraint violations. In this paper, we extend the indirect feedback approach for linear stochastic MPC from chance constraints to risk-averse constraints like the conditional value-at-risk. For the resulting risk-averse MPC scheme, we establish recursive feasibility and closed-loop constraint satisfaction. Furthermore, based on a stochastic dissipativity notion and suitable conditions on the terminal ingredients we show that (near)-optimality of the averaged closed-loop performance can be ensured.

</details>


### [75] [Gradient extremals, talwegs, valleys, and directional alignment for generic gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11213)
*Pascal Bégout,Jérôme Bolte,Thomas Mariotti,Francisco Silva*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究梯度极值线（gradient extremals）的几何性质及其在梯度流动力学中的作用，包括方向对齐、收敛速率和体积集中现象


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度极值线作为梯度是Hessian特征向量的轨迹，为理解山谷（valleys）和谷底线（talwegs）等几何概念提供了自然框架。研究这些对象有助于深入理解梯度流及其离散版本的动力学行为

Method: 采用变分观点分析梯度极值线，研究梯度流及其离散对应轨迹与梯度极值线切空间的方向对齐性。在非共振假设下分析对齐速率，并证明体积集中现象

Result: 梯度流轨迹与梯度极值线切空间存在方向对齐，在非共振情况下对齐速率由第一谱间隙或Hessian最小特征值决定。非线性性和步长会复杂地扭曲这些速率。证明了体积集中现象：长时间后初始条件集的图像集中在山谷内并渐近收敛于谷底线

Conclusion: 梯度极值线在梯度流动力学中具有结构化作用，为理解优化景观的几何性质提供了重要框架。这些结果揭示了梯度下降算法在非凸优化中的几何行为特征

Abstract: Gradient extremals are loci along which the gradient is an eigenvector of the Hessian. These objects provide a natural geometric framework connecting several notions, notably valleys and talwegs, which we analyze from a variational viewpoint in the generic case. We then show that trajectories of the gradient flow and of its discrete counterpart exhibit directional alignment with the tangent spaces to gradient extremals, and generically to the talweg. Under non-resonance assumptions, and in contrast with the quadratic case, alignment rates are governed either by the first spectral gap or by the smallest eigenvalue of the Hessian at the limit point. Nonlinearities and the step length may both distort these rates in a complex manner. We further prove a volume concentration phenomenon emphasizing the structuring role of gradient extremals: for large times, the images of sets of initial conditions concentrate inside valleys and asymptotically around talwegs.

</details>


### [76] [Data-driven balanced truncation of K-power bilinear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11268)
*Xiaolong Wang,Biaolin Li,Xiaoli Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对K-power双线性系统的数据驱动平衡截断方法，通过传递函数采样数据近似构建降阶模型，避免了传统方法对系统矩阵的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-power双线性系统具有特殊的耦合结构和良好的实际性质，但传统的平衡截断方法需要完整的系统矩阵信息。研究旨在开发一种数据驱动的方法，仅通过传递函数采样数据就能实现平衡截断降阶。

Method: 利用K-power系统的Gramian矩阵特性，建立平衡截断主要量与传递函数采样值之间的显式关系。通过传递函数采样数据近似每个子系统的降阶量，然后组装成完整的降阶模型。还提供了避免复数运算、生成实值降阶模型的高级流程。

Result: 建立了平衡截断量与传递函数采样之间的数学关系，实现了完全基于数据驱动的平衡截断方法。通过两个数值算例验证了所提方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 成功开发了针对K-power双线性系统的数据驱动平衡截断方法，该方法仅需传递函数采样数据即可构建降阶模型，避免了传统方法对系统矩阵的依赖，为实际工程应用提供了更灵活的降阶工具。

Abstract: As a special type of bilinear systems, K-power bilinear systems possess a special coupled structure along with nice properties in practice. In this paper, we investigate the data-driven counterpart of balanced truncation for K-power systems. As the standard balanced truncation is performed based on the subsystems of K-power systems, the main idea is to approximate the quantities of each reduced subsystem with the evaluations of transfer functions. We exploit the nice properties of Gramians for K-power systems, and establish the explicit relationship between the main quantities of balanced truncation and the evaluation of transfer functions. As a result, reduced models produced via balanced truncation can be assembled approximately by the sample data of transfer functions, leading to a data-driven balancing truncation method for K-power systems. An advanced procedure is also provided to avoid the complex arithmetic completely and produce real-valued reduced models. Two numerical examples confirm the feasibility and effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [77] [Incentive Design without Hypergradients: A Social-Gradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11346)
*Georgios Vasileiou,Lantian Zhang,Silun Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需超梯度的激励设计方法——社会梯度流，用于信息不对称下引导自私个体达到社会最优纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统激励设计方法需要计算或近似超梯度（总导数），这通常需要了解均衡对激励的敏感性，但在信息不对称（个体成本函数不确定）下难以获得。需要一种不依赖超梯度的激励设计方法。

Method: 提出社会梯度流激励法则，利用社会成本梯度作为下降方向。在均衡可观测的理想情况下直接应用；在均衡不可观测时，采用双时间尺度交互：个体策略在快时间尺度演化，激励在慢时间尺度更新。证明社会成本梯度总是规划者目标函数的下降方向。

Result: 在均衡可观测的理想情况下，社会梯度流收敛到唯一的社会最优激励。在均衡不可观测时，双时间尺度交互的联合策略-激励动态收敛到社会最优，只要个体学习规则能渐近跟踪均衡。数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 社会梯度流为信息不对称下的激励设计提供了一种无需超梯度的有效方法，在均衡可观测和不可观测两种情况下都能收敛到社会最优，具有理论保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: Incentive design problems consider a system planner who steers self-interested agents toward a socially optimal Nash equilibrium by issuing incentives in the presence of information asymmetry, that is, uncertainty about the agents' cost functions. A common approach formulates the problem as a Mathematical Program with Equilibrium Constraints (MPEC) and optimizes incentives using hypergradients-the total derivatives of the planner's objective with respect to incentives. However, computing or approximating the hypergradients typically requires full or partial knowledge of equilibrium sensitivities to incentives, which is generally unavailable under information asymmetry. In this paper, we propose a hypergradient-free incentive law, called the social-gradient flow, for incentive design when the planner's social cost depends on the agents' joint actions. We prove that the social cost gradient is always a descent direction for the planner's objective, irrespective of the agent cost landscape. In the idealized setting where equilibrium responses are observable, the social-gradient flow converges to the unique socially optimal incentive. When equilibria are not directly observable, the social-gradient flow emerges as the slow-timescale limit of a two-timescale interaction, in which agents' strategies evolve on a faster timescale. It is established that the joint strategy-incentive dynamics converge to the social optimum for any agent learning rule that asymptotically tracks the equilibrium. Theoretical results are also validated via numerical experiments.

</details>


### [78] [Characterization of law-invariant BSDEs and dynamic risk measures in continuous time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11382)
*Zakaria Bensaid,Roxana Dumitrescu,Anis Matoussi,Wissal Sabbagh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提供了具有二次增长率的定律不变BSDEs的新特征化，解决了Xu-Xu-Zhou (2022)中关于g-期望定律不变性必要条件的开放性问题，并将分析扩展到一般（可能非确定性）生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Xu-Xu-Zhou (2022)中提出的关于g-期望定律不变性必要条件的开放性问题，将定律不变性的分析扩展到更一般的BSDEs框架，特别是具有二次增长率的生成元。

Method: 通过提供新的特征化方法来分析定律不变BSDEs，引入并比较连续时间中新的动态定律不变性概念，将结果应用于一类强时间一致性的动态风险度量。

Result: 成功解决了Xu-Xu-Zhou (2022)中的开放性问题，为具有二次增长率的定律不变BSDEs提供了完整的特征化，并将分析扩展到非确定性生成元的情况。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了定律不变性在BSDEs理论中的应用，超越了文献中离散时间现金可加且归一化动态凸风险度量的特征化，为动态风险度量的理论发展提供了新工具。

Abstract: We provide a new characterization of law-invariant BSDEs with quadratic growth. This answers the open question raised in Xu-Xu-Zhou (2022) [44] on necessary conditions for law-invariance of g-expectations and extends the analysis to general (possibly non-deterministic) generators. In continuous time, we also introduce and compare new dynamic notions of law-invariance. As a byproduct, we apply our results to a large class of strongly time-consistent dynamic risk measures. In particular, our results go beyond the discrete-time characterization of cash-additive and normalized dynamic convex risk measures studied in the literature, notably in Kupper-Schachermayer (2009) [33].

</details>


### [79] [Q-quadratic convergence of the centralized circumcentered-reflection method under a relative interior condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11450)
*Yunier Bello-Cruz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了集中式外心反射方法（cCRM）在仿射子空间相等（aff(X)=aff(Y)）情况下的收敛性，证明了在相对边界满足C¹光滑性时具有超线性收敛，在C²光滑时具有Q-二次收敛，并给出了显式渐近常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有cCRM方法在X∩Y内部非空且边界为C¹超曲面时具有超线性收敛性，但当aff(X)=aff(Y)⊊ℝⁿ时（如等式约束可行性和谱矩阵问题），这些条件不成立。需要研究cCRM在仿射子空间相等情况下的收敛行为。

Method: 通过分析相对边界的光滑性条件，在aff(X)=aff(Y)且相对内部交集非空的情况下，研究cCRM的收敛性。使用边界曲率和局部误差界常数来表征渐近收敛速度。

Result: 当aff(X)=aff(Y)，相对内部交集非空，且相对边界为适当相对维度的C¹光滑时，cCRM具有超线性收敛性；当相对边界为C²光滑时，具有Q-二次收敛性，并给出了用极限点处边界曲率和局部误差界常数表示的显式渐近常数。

Conclusion: 论文扩展了cCRM的收敛理论到仿射子空间相等的情况，填补了现有理论的空白，并识别了aff(X)≠aff(Y)的情况作为开放问题。

Abstract: The centralized circumcentered-reflection method (\cCRM) of~\cite{Behling:2024} converges superlinearly to a solution of $\operatorname{find}\;z\in X\cap Y$ when $\inte(X\cap Y)\neq\emptyset$ and the boundaries of $X$ and $Y$ are $\mathcal{C}^1$ hypersurfaces in $\re^n$. Both conditions fail when $\aff(X)=\aff(Y)\subsetneq\re^n$, as in equality-constrained feasibility and spectral matrix problems. We prove that \cCRM\ converges superlinearly when $\aff(X)=\aff(Y)$, $\operatorname{ri}(X)\cap\operatorname{ri}(Y)\neq\emptyset$, and the relative boundaries are $\mathcal{C}^1$ of appropriate relative dimension; and Q-quadratically when the relative boundaries are $\mathcal{C}^2$, with explicit asymptotic constant expressed in terms of the boundary curvatures at the limit point and the local error-bound constant. The case $\aff(X)\neq\aff(Y)$ is identified as open.

</details>


### [80] [Risk-Constrained Kelly for Mutually Exclusive Outcomes: CRRA Support Invariance and Logarithmic One-Dimensional Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11577)
*Christopher D. Long*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究有限互斥结果下的风险约束凯利优化问题，在标准超额赔率机制下，风险约束会改变财富分布但不改变活跃状态集


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在风险约束条件下的凯利优化问题，特别是在超额赔率机制下，探索风险约束如何影响投资组合的优化结果，特别是活跃状态集是否保持不变

Method: 使用恒定相对风险厌恶效用函数，在超额赔率机制下分析风险约束优化问题；证明在无约束问题的唯一似然比前缀假设下，约束优化器具有相同的活跃集；特别分析对数效用情况，建立校准定理和结构化算法

Result: 证明在标准超额赔率机制下，风险约束优化器与无约束优化器具有完全相同的活跃状态集；风险约束会改变财富分布但不改变支持集；在对数效用情况下，建立了存在性、唯一性和单调性结果，并提供了完整的校准算法

Conclusion: 在超额赔率机制下，风险约束仅改变财富分布而不改变活跃状态集；支持集在CRRA参数和风险约束参数下保持不变；对数效用情况有完整的理论解和算法；公平和次公平机制需要不同处理

Abstract: We study the finite mutually exclusive outcome version of risk-constrained Kelly optimization with explicit state prices. The market has outcome probabilities $p_i>0$, state prices $q_i>0$, terminal wealths $W_i=c+x_i/q_i$, and a drawdown-surrogate constraint \[ \sum_{i=1}^n p_i W_i^{-λ}\le 1,\qquad λ>0. \] For constant relative risk aversion utility, we work primarily in the standard overround regime $\sum_i q_i>1$, where every optimizer is necessarily non-full-support. Under the usual unique likelihood-ratio prefix hypothesis for the unconstrained problem, we prove that the constrained optimizer has exactly the same active set. Thus, in the regime where the prefix theorem is meaningful, the risk constraint deforms the funded wealth profile but does not change the active set. The support is therefore invariant across both the CRRA parameter and the drawdown-surrogate parameter.
  We then isolate the logarithmic case $γ=1$. Once the common active prefix is known, the constrained problem reduces to a one-dimensional outer calibration together with independent one-dimensional inner equations on the active states. In this case we prove existence, uniqueness, and monotonicity for the inner solves, derive a complete calibration theorem, and record the resulting structured algorithm. We treat the fair and subfair regimes only as boundary cases: full-support phenomena can occur there, so the overround prefix theory no longer yields a parallel exact description of comparable sharpness. A numerical example illustrates how the risk constraint alters the funded wealth profile while leaving support unchanged.

</details>


### [81] [Computation of Least Trimmed Squares: A Branch-and-Bound framework with Hyperplane Arrangement Enhancements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11584)
*Xiang Meng,Andrés Gómez,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的混合整数优化（MIO）公式，用于解决惩罚最小修剪平方（LTS）回归问题，通过超平面排列逻辑嵌入透视重构，显著提高了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惩罚LTS回归是稳健统计中的关键问题，能有效减少异常值影响，但现有MIO方法由于松弛性弱和指数级复杂度，在大规模数据集上计算效率低下。

Method: 提出新的MIO公式，将超平面排列逻辑嵌入透视重构，显式强制执行最优解的结构特性；开发定制分支定界算法，使用一阶方法和对偶界高效求解节点松弛。

Result: 在特征数固定时，分支定界树规模是样本量的多项式；在5000样本、20特征的合成数据上，1分钟内达到1%间隙，而现有方法1小时内无法完成。

Conclusion: 新方法显著提升了惩罚LTS回归的计算效率，使在低维设置下处理更大样本量的精确稳健回归成为可能。

Abstract: We study computational aspects of a key problem in robust statistics -- the penalized least trimmed squares (LTS) regression problem, a robust estimator that mitigates the influence of outliers in data by capping residuals with large magnitudes. Although statistically attractive, penalized LTS is NP-hard, and existing mixed-integer optimization (MIO) formulations scale poorly due to weak relaxations and exponential worst-case complexity in the number of observations. We propose a new MIO formulation that embeds hyperplane arrangement logic into a perspective reformulation, explicitly enforcing structural properties of optimal solutions. We show that, if the number of features is fixed, the resulting branch-and-bound tree is of polynomial size in the sample size. Moreover, we develop a tailored branch-and-bound algorithm that uses first-order methods with dual bounds to solve node relaxations efficiently. Computational experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate substantial improvements over existing MIO approaches: on synthetic instances with 5000 samples and 20 features, our tailored solver reaches a 1% gap in 1 minute while competing approaches fail to do so within one hour. These gains enable exact robust regression at significantly larger sample sizes in low-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [82] [A Distributed Bilevel Framework for the Macroscopic Optimization of Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11712)
*Riccardo Brumali,Guido Carnevale,Sonia Martínez,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式双层优化算法，通过微观行动优化大规模多智能体系统的宏观涌现行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在大规模多智能体系统中，通过微观层面的个体行动来优化系统整体的宏观涌现行为，这是一个具有挑战性的控制问题

Method: 将任务建模为双层优化问题：上层通过性能准则形式化期望的宏观目标行为，下层利用压缩的聚合表示估计宏观状态来塑造行为。宏观状态由指数族分布参数化，从多智能体微观配置构建。算法集成分布式估计机制（每个智能体本地重建宏观状态）和基于超梯度的微观状态更新

Result: 通过时间尺度分离论证证明了算法收敛到双层问题的平稳点集，数值模拟验证了所提方法的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的分布式算法能够有效优化大规模多智能体系统的宏观涌现行为，通过双层优化框架和分布式估计机制实现了从微观行动到宏观行为的协调控制

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel distributed algorithm to optimize the emergent macroscopic behavior of large-scale multi-agent systems via microscopic actions. We cast this task as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper level formalizes the desired macroscopic target behavior through a suitable performance criterion, which is shaped in the lower level by leveraging a compressed aggregate representation estimating the macroscopic state. More precisely, the macroscopic state is parametrized by an exponential-family of distributions and constructed from the multi-agent microscopic configuration. The proposed algorithm integrates a distributed estimation mechanism, through which each agent reconstructs the macroscopic state locally, with a hypergradient-based update of the microscopic states aimed at improving the collective macroscopic behavior. We prove convergence to the set of stationary points of the bilevel problem via timescale separation arguments. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [83] [Informativity for Data-driven Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11726)
*Joel Stevens,Jeremy Coulson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了数据驱动预测问题，针对未知动态的LTI系统，使用系统收集的数据来预测给定已知输入序列的系统输出响应，提出了比现有方法更弱的条件来实现预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的数据驱动预测方法需要强条件（如持续激励），限制了实际应用。本文旨在寻找更弱的条件，使预测在更广泛的情况下成为可能。

Method: 从数据信息性角度出发，形式化定义了唯一预测的信息性概念，提供了唯一预测信息性的充分条件，并设计了算法来计算未知系统在给定已知输入下的唯一输出轨迹。

Result: 证明了在不需要唯一识别未知数据生成系统的条件下，仍然可以实现唯一输出预测，并通过数值示例展示了这一结果。

Conclusion: 本文为数据驱动预测提供了更弱的条件框架，扩展了预测的可能性，展示了预测与系统识别之间的分离性。

Abstract: In this work we examine the problem of data-driven prediction. That is, given a LTI system with unknown dynamics, we wish to use data collected from the system to predict the system's output response to a given sequence of known inputs. Current methods for predicting require strong conditions on the data such as persistency of excitation. We examine this problem with the goal of finding weaker conditions that still enable prediction. We approach the problem from the data informativity perspective and formally define the notion of informativity for unique prediction. We provide sufficient conditions for informativity for unique prediction and design algorithms to compute the unique output trajectory of the unknown system given known inputs. We demonstrate the results with a numerical example showing that unique output prediction is possible without being able to uniquely identify the unknown data-generating system.

</details>


### [84] [Fairness-aware Strategic Design of Station-based Electric Car-Sharing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11732)
*Jue Zhou,Zoha Sherkat-Masoumi,Merve Bodur*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个公平导向的优化框架，用于设计电动汽车共享系统，同时考虑经济收益和服务公平性，通过分支定价算法和精确双目标方法生成帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电动汽车共享系统设计模型主要关注经济效益，忽视了服务公平性这一关键维度。充电需求和运营约束使得系统设计复杂化，需要将长期投资决策与日常运营现实相结合，同时确保不同用户群体获得公平服务。

Method: 采用两种公平性范式（服务率差异和最大最小公平），基于多天代表性需求设置，建立双目标轨迹模型联合优化收益和服务公平性。开发分支定价算法求解单目标变体，嵌入精确双目标过程生成帕累托前沿，并采用潜水启发式方法处理大规模实例。

Result: 通过维也纳真实数据案例研究，揭示了收益、公平性和系统设计之间的基本权衡关系。提出的框架能够生成高质量帕累托前沿近似解，为决策者提供有效的决策支持工具。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了电动汽车共享系统设计中公平性考虑的空白，提出的优化框架能够设计出既经济可行又具有社会包容性的站点式电动汽车共享系统，为城市可持续交通提供重要决策支持。

Abstract: Electric car-sharing systems are pivotal for sustainable urban mobility, but their strategic design is complicated by operational constraints, particularly those arising from the charging needs of electric vehicles. The success of these systems hinges on integrating long-term investment decisions (such as station locations, charger capacities, and fleet size) with daily operational realities, including vehicle routing to serve user trip requests and battery management. While existing integrated models address this strategic-operational link, they have prioritized economic efficiency, overlooking the critical dimension of service equity. This paper addresses this gap by making fairness a central design principle, operationalized through two distinct paradigms, namely, service-rate disparity and max-min fairness, measured explicitly via realized group service rates rather than static spatial accessibility. To capture demand heterogeneity, we adopt a multi-day representative-demand setting, and develop a bi-objective trajectory-based formulation that jointly optimizes revenue and service equity. We develop a solution framework in which a branch-and-price algorithm solves the single-objective variants of the models, embedded within an exact bi-objective procedure to generate the Pareto frontier and complemented by a diving-heuristic-based approach for obtaining high-quality frontier approximations for larger instances. Through extensive computational experiments, including a Vienna-based real-data case study, we provide key managerial insights into the fundamental trade-offs between revenue, equity, and system design, demonstrating that the proposed framework can serve as a useful decision-support tool for designing station-based electric car-sharing systems that are both economically viable and socially inclusive.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [LABBench2: An Improved Benchmark for AI Systems Performing Biology Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09554)
*Jon M Laurent,Albert Bou,Michael Pieler,Conor Igoe,Alex Andonian,Siddharth Narayanan,James Braza,Alexandros Sanchez Vassopoulos,Jacob L Steenwyk,Blake Lash,Andrew D White,Samuel G Rodriques*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LABBench2是LAB-Bench基准的进化版本，包含近1900个任务，用于更真实地评估AI系统在科学任务中的实际能力，结果显示新基准难度显著提升（准确率下降26%-46%），为AI科学能力发展提供了更严格的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在科学发现中应用的快速增长，需要从测量基础知识和推理能力转向评估AI系统执行实际科学工作的能力。LAB-Bench作为初步尝试，需要进化为更贴近真实科学场景的评估基准。

Method: 开发LABBench2基准，包含近1900个任务，延续LAB-Bench的评估维度但置于更真实的科学场景中。评估当前前沿模型性能，比较新旧基准的难度差异，并提供公开数据集和评估工具。

Result: 相比LAB-Bench，LABBench2难度显著提升：模型在各项子任务上的准确率下降26%到46%。虽然AI系统的科学能力已有显著进步，但新基准揭示了仍有巨大的性能提升空间。

Conclusion: LABBench2作为LAB-Bench的进化版本，为评估AI系统在真实科学任务中的能力提供了更严格的基准，将继续推动AI科学工具的发展，并作为该领域的实际标准基准。

Abstract: Optimism for accelerating scientific discovery with AI continues to grow. Current applications of AI in scientific research range from training dedicated foundation models on scientific data to agentic autonomous hypothesis generation systems to AI-driven autonomous labs. The need to measure progress of AI systems in scientific domains correspondingly must not only accelerate, but increasingly shift focus to more real-world capabilities. Beyond rote knowledge and even just reasoning to actually measuring the ability to perform meaningful work. Prior work introduced the Language Agent Biology Benchmark LAB-Bench as an initial attempt at measuring these abilities. Here we introduce an evolution of that benchmark, LABBench2, for measuring real-world capabilities of AI systems performing useful scientific tasks. LABBench2 comprises nearly 1,900 tasks and is, for the most part, a continuation of LAB-Bench, measuring similar capabilities but in more realistic contexts. We evaluate performance of current frontier models, and show that while abilities measured by LAB-Bench and LABBench2 have improved substantially, LABBench2 provides a meaningful jump in difficulty (model-specific accuracy differences range from -26% to -46% across subtasks) and underscores continued room for performance improvement. LABBench2 continues the legacy of LAB-Bench as a de facto benchmark for AI scientific research capabilities and we hope that it continues to help advance development of AI tools for these core research functions. To facilitate community use and development, we provide the task dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/futurehouse/labbench2 and a public eval harness at https://github.com/EdisonScientific/labbench2.

</details>


### [86] [Linear Programming for Multi-Criteria Assessment with Cardinal and Ordinal Data: A Pessimistic Virtual Gap Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09555)
*Fuh-Hwa Franklin Liu,Su-Chuan Shih*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于虚拟间隙分析的两步法，通过悲观视角评估多准则决策中的备选方案，使用定量/定性准则和基数/序数数据，优先排除最差方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多准则分析方法存在主观评价和偏见影响结果可靠性，数据多样性影响参数精度的问题，需要更可靠、可扩展的评估方法。

Method: 提出两步法：1) 集成两种新型虚拟间隙分析模型，从悲观视角评估备选方案，处理定量/定性准则和基数/序数数据；2) 优先排除最不利方案。

Result: 该方法可靠且可扩展，能够在决策支持系统中高效有效地进行全面评估。

Conclusion: 基于线性规划的虚拟间隙分析模型能有效解决多准则决策中的主观偏见和数据多样性问题，提供更可靠的评估框架。
